lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/RingTheory/WittVector/StructurePolynomial.lean:is the same as evaluating `Φ` on the (appropriately renamed) `n`-th Witt polynomials.
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/RingTheory/WittVector/StructurePolynomial.lean:For example, by taking `Φ = X tt + X ff` resp. `Φ = X tt * X ff`
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/RingTheory/WittVector/StructurePolynomial.lean:We therefore obtain a family of polynomials `wittStructureRat Φ`
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/RingTheory/WittVector/StructurePolynomial.lean:for every `Φ : MvPolynomial idx ℚ`.
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/RingTheory/WittVector/StructurePolynomial.lean:If `Φ` has integer coefficients, then the polynomials `wittStructureRat Φ n` do so as well.
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/RingTheory/WittVector/StructurePolynomial.lean:of `wittStructureInt Φ n` from the integers to the rationals,
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/RingTheory/WittVector/StructurePolynomial.lean:one obtains `wittStructureRat Φ n`.
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/RingTheory/WittVector/StructurePolynomial.lean:* `wittStructureRat Φ`: the family of polynomials `ℕ → MvPolynomial (idx × ℕ) ℚ`
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/RingTheory/WittVector/StructurePolynomial.lean:  associated with `Φ : MvPolynomialidx ℚ` and satisfying the property explained above.
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/RingTheory/WittVector/StructurePolynomial.lean:* `wittStructureInt Φ`: the family of polynomials `ℕ → MvPolynomial (idx × ℕ) ℤ`
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/RingTheory/WittVector/StructurePolynomial.lean:  associated with `Φ : MvPolynomialidx ℤ` and satisfying the property explained above.

lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/Sites/Point/Basic.lean:structure `GrothendieckTopology.Point`. Sucha `Φ : J.Point` consists
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/Sites/Point/Basic.lean:in a functor `Φ.fiber : C ⥤ Type w` such that the category `Φ.fiber.Elements`
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/Sites/Point/Basic.lean:is cofiltered (and initially small) and suchthat if `x : Φ.fiber.obj X`
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/Sites/Point/Basic.lean:of some `y : Φ.fiber.obj Y` by a morphism `f: Y ⟶ X` which belongs to `R`.

lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/Sites/Point/Basic.lean:`Φ.fiber.Elementsᵒᵖ`. We define `Φ.sheafFiber : Sheaf J A ⥤ A` as the
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/Sites/Point/Basic.lean:restriction of `Φ.presheafFiber` to the fullsubcategory of sheaves.
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/Sites/Point/Basic.lean:that not only `Φ.sheafFiber : Sheaf J A ⥤ A`is the restriction of
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/Sites/Point/Basic.lean:`Φ.presheafFiber` but it may also be thoughtas a localization
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/Sites/Point/Basic.lean:both `Φ.presheafFiber` and `Φ.sheafFiber` commute with finite limits
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/Sites/Point/Basic.lean:and with arbitrary colimits. (The commutation of `Φ.sheafFiber` with colimits

lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/SmallObject/TransfiniteIteration.lean:Given a successor structure `: SuccStruct C`
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/SmallObject/TransfiniteIteration.lean:and a well-ordered type `J`, we define the iteration `Φ.iteration J : C`. It is
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/SmallObject/TransfiniteIteration.lean:defined as the colimit of a functor `Φ.iterationFunctor J : J ⥤ C`.
lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/SmallObject/TransfiniteIteration.lean:variable {C : Type u} [Category.{v} C] (Φ : SuccStruct C)

lean4/.lake/packages/mathlib/Mathlib/CategoryTheory/SmallObject/TransfiniteIteration.lean:is the functor `J ⥤ C` which gives the iterations of `Φ` indexed by `J`. -/
